# Prompt used at query #1
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #16
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string

import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"

    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")

    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e

    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #60
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #75
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #119
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack

def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:

# Prompt used at query #134
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

# Prompt used at query #178
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e

    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"

    return s

    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.

    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")

    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #38
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #53
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

from __future__ import annotations

import base64

    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"

    return s

    safe to use in URLs.

    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")

    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e

    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #97
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #112
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

# Prompt used at query #156
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

Write unit test with pytest for function base64_decode with the following signature: `def test_base64_decode():`

_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

# Prompt used at query #171
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import string
import struct
import typing as t

from .exc import BadData


def want_bytes(
    s: str | bytes, encoding: str = "utf-8", errors: str = "strict"
) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(s, str):
        s = s.encode(encoding, errors)

    return s


def base64_encode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 encode a string of bytes or text. The resulting bytes are
    safe to use in URLs.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string)
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(string).rstrip(b"=")


def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]

def base64_decode(string: str | bytes) -> bytes:
    """Base64 decode a URL-safe string of bytes or text. The result is
    bytes.
    """
    string = want_bytes(string, encoding="ascii", errors="ignore")
    string += b"=" * (-len(string) % 4)

    try:
        return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(string)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise BadData("Invalid base64-encoded data") from e


# The alphabet used by base64.urlsafe_*
_base64_alphabet = f"{string.ascii_letters}{string.digits}-_=".encode("ascii")

_int64_struct = struct.Struct(">Q")
_int_to_bytes = _int64_struct.pack
_bytes_to_int = t.cast("t.Callable[[bytes], tuple[int]]", _int64_struct.unpack)


def int_to_bytes(num: int) -> bytes:
    return _int_to_bytes(num).lstrip(b"\x00")


def bytes_to_int(bytestr: bytes) -> int:
    return _bytes_to_int(bytestr.rjust(8, b"\x00"))[0]
